___ जहां इस अधिसूचना द्वारा कोई प्राय -कर सकिल , वार्ड या जिला या 
उमका कोई भाग एक रेंज से दूमरी रेंज को अन्तरित हो गया हो वहां 
उस पाय-कर सकिल पाई या जिले या उसके किसी भाग में किए गए 
निर्धारणों के परिणामस्वरूप की गई अपीले , जो इस अधिसूचना की तारीन 
से टीक पहले उस रेंज के , जिससे यह प्राय -कर सकिल, वार्ड या जिले 
या उसका कोई भाग अंतरित कर दिया गया है, सहायक प्रायुक्त ( अपील ) 
के ममक्ष लम्बित थी , इस अधिसूचना के प्रभावी होने की तारीख से उस 
रेंज के , जिमको उक्त सर्फिल वाई या जिला या उसका कोई भाग अंतरित कर 
दिया गया है , सहायक आयुक्स ( अपील ) को अंतरित कर दी जाएंगी 
जो उनके संबंध में कार्यवाही करेगा । 


15. सहायक आयुक्त ( अपील ) "क " 

रेंज, रोहतक । 


1 . क - बाई , रोहतक । 
2 . कम्पनी वार्ड, रोहतक । 
3. आयकर अधिकारी, फरीदाबाद 
4. क - याई, हिसार । 
5 . क -याएं, सिरमा । 


यह अधिसूचना 1- 9-1972 से प्रभावी होगी ।

जहा इस अधिसूचना द्वारा कोई आयकर मर्किल, वार्ड या जिला या

Where an Income- tax Circle , Ward or District or part thereof

का प्रा० 2127 - -केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 233 
के अधीन निहित शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए प्रधोहस्ताक्षरकर्ता मारा

निर्माण में प्रयोग की जाने वाली कच्ची सामग्री यथा- -- स्पिलंट्स और पीनर्स , 
गन्धक तथा पोटासियम क्लोरेट का, संलग्न प्रपत्र मे पृथक दैनिक लेखा रखेंगे । 
ऐसे निर्माता केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 55 के अधीन 
प्रपत्र प्रार० टी० 5 मे उस कम्ची सामग्री की त्रैमासिक विवरणी भी 
अनुवर्ती त्रैमाम के प्रथम 10 दिनों के भीतर केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क के 
सम्बन्धित क्षेत्राधिकारी ( रेंज आफिमर ) को भेजेगे तथा उसकी प्रतिया 
केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क के सम्बन्धित सहायक समाहर्ता को भी भेजेगे । 

सलग्न कच्ची सामग्री के वैनिक लेखा का प्रपत्र । ।

1 - 2 - 1969, Miss P . A . Mary on 18 - 12 - 1969 and Shri Bipin R .

loyed through tho Employment Exchange ; that they have 
long back coniplcted more than 240 days of their service and

larised . The union has submitted that the Group Gathering 
Station and other telephone cxchange on which they are 
employed , is of a permanent nature ; that they are not tem 
porary installations , that the fact that the said employees 
are on the said installations for about 3 years shows that the 
same is of permanent nature ; that they are regularly attending 
on the said installation as regular employces and they are 
not workcharged employecs ; that their work is also not of 
casual naturo ; that in the circumstances denying them the 
benefits that are admissible to the regular employees is an 
act of grave injustice . The union has submitted that under 
the Industrial Employment (Standing Orders ) Act, 1946 , tho 
management are rcquired to define the conditions of service ; 
that there is no standing order in respect of workcharged 
employees ; that if these are workcharged employees their 
conditions of service must be formally defined ; that the 
management is not entitled to treat them as workcharged emp 
loyees and must be directed to treat them as permanent 
employees .

There docs not appear to me any doubt whatsoever that 
the duties performed by these persons are similar to the duties 
performed by telephone operators and that the work which 
they are doing is of a permanent nature . After tho reference 
was made an agreement has been arrived at between the Oll 
& Natural Gas Commission and ONGC Employees Mazdoor 
Sabha regarding regularisation of casual / contingent /work 
charged / ad hoc employees who have been working in the 
Western region for a considerable period . Under the same, 
the ONGC has agreed to create a total of 141 regular posts 
in various trades and this inclụdes tho posts of 6 telephone 
Operators . It has also further filed a written statement before 
mo inter alia in which they have stated that a settlement has 
been arrived at between the Chairman , Oil & Natural Gas 
Commission and the Oil & Natural Gas Commission Emp 
loyees Mazdoor Sabha whereby the Oil & Natural Gas 
Commission has agreed to create 141 regular posts in various 
trades including the 6 posts of telephone operators and the 
persons covered in this reference are also covered under the 
said settlement , dated 14 - 4 - 1972 and that out of 6 persons 
In the settlement 5 are also covered in this referenco ; that 
they are therefore following the further procedure for regu 
lanising the posts of telephone operators under its existing 
rules , i. e ., character verification , polico record , medical exami 
nation etc ., that since the said settlement has been arrived at 
with the majority union I should only make an award in 
terms of the said settlement particularly when a number of 
other persons are also involved . Tho settlement itself relied 
upon by the Commission conclusively shows that there was 
necessity of 6 permanent posts of telephone operators . I 
would therefore , be perfectly justified in regularişing the posts 
of the 5 telephone operators before me subject to the terms 
laid down therein . In fact, in view of that settlement the 
demand must be granted . 


The company in their written statement have donied many 
Allegations made by the union . It has submitted that tho 
employees concerned in the refcrence were taken in employ 
ment on workcharged basis and by the terms of appointment 
they have no right to claim the benefit of regular employees 
and thut looking to the terms of their appointment tho 
demand is not legally maintainable . It has further been sub 
mitted that there are no permanent posts so far created by 
the Commission against which the employees can be absorbed 
as regular cniployecs ; that till tho permanent posts are created 
by the Commission or the permanent posts become vacant 
there can be no question of absorbing the employees as 
regular employees or on regular basis and that the demand 
should , therefore , be rejected . It has further been submitted 
that the demand involves a question of promotion ; that the 
considerations for employing persons on workcharged basis 
are different from those for employing the porsons on perma 
nent or regular basis ; that the qualifications , suitability , ex 
perience etc . were scrutinised and judged vis- a -vis employees 
on temporary or workcharged basis and that the same Wero 
not considered vis - a - vis the employment on permanent or 
regular basis ; that in the circumstances it would not be just 
and appropriate to direct the Commission to absorb them on 
regular or permanent basis . 


The stand taken in the written statement hurdly seems 
to be proper in a dispute of this kind . It may be that thoy 
were taken in eroployment on workcharged basis and the 
fore by thc terms of appointment they may hayo no right 
to claim the benefits of regular employces but that is the 
very reason why this dispute is raised . If persons are emp 
loyed as workcharged employecs when , in fact , the work on 
which they are cmployed , is of a permanent nature , that 
itself would be a proper ground for interference by this Tri 
bunal. The terms of appointment also would be irrelevant . 
The reply suggests a complete ignorance of the scope and func 
tions of industrial adjudication , Again , it is stated that at 
the time of their appointment the considerations for employing 
them and the qualifications for omploying them were scen vis 
a - vis those of the teniporary Or workcharged employces but 
no effort is made to indicate what the qualifications etc . are 
vis - a - vis permanent employces . The reply of the Commis 
sion makes no effort to rebut the allegations that the Commis 
sion could not cm hese persons as workcharged cmployees . 
It also makes no effort to reply to their allegation that the 
work on which they were employed was work of a perma 
nent nature and thoy were therefore wrongly employed as 
workcharged employees. The reply of the Commission leaves 
much to bo desired and it does not meet the casc made out 
by the workmen in their statemont of claim . An affidavit bas 
been filed before me by one Shumbhu Prasud Mitbani which , 
inter alia , states how he was selected and that ho has been 
continuously working as telephone operator over since he 
was appointed ; that he had been doing all the regular duties 
of a telephone operator , that the nature of work performed 
by him is also of a permanent paturo ; that there is only one 
telephone exchangc at GGS- 2 which is a permanent installa 
tion and there are no other ONGC telephone exchange in 
the area . He has also stated that Shri Ashok Kaduskar , 
Shri Bhika Bhai Thakkar and Shri Bipin R . Thakkar are also 


The other question is the dato from which these persons 
should be regularised and the conditions on which they should 
be regularised . Tho agreement is silent as to the date . The 
practice , according to the Commission , in to regularise tho 
employees from the date they were regularised and the Com 
Mission desires that I should not pass any other order . 

The demand on behalf of these workmen was made by the 
ONGC Employees Union of whom they are members and 
when their request was turned down they brought it to the 
notice of the assistant Labour Commissioner (Central ) by a 
letter, dated 17th April, 1971 , and called for his intervention . 
The conciliation mceting was called by him on 30th April , 
1971. On failuro this reference has been made on 8 - 12 - 1971. 
Out of these persons, 3 of them were appointed on 1 - 2 - 1969, 
one of them on 18 - 12 - 1969 and one of them on 1 - 7 - 1969 
more than four years back . In the circumstances of the case , 
I do not think I would be justificd in directing that they bo 
regularised prospectively from the date the enquiry otc. 18 
Over or from the date when they entered into an agreement 
with tho majority union of which they are not the memberg . 
Their case was being agitated by the ONGC Employees 
Union very much earlier . The matter had even gone in con 
ciliation and there hardly appears to be any justification when 
tho work for which they were employed was of a permanent 
nature for continuing them as workcharged employees . How 
ever , I do not think I would be justificd in dispensing with 
the nocessary procedure before making persons regularised , 
i.e ., character verification , police records, inedical examina 
tion , etc . I ani , however , given to understand that with con 
siderable effort the character verification and police records 
have already been completed in the case of these persons and 
what remians is only medical examination . I, therefore , direct 
as follows : 


( 1) That the Commission shall within one month from the 
date this award becomes enforceable completo character 
verification medical examination and other necessary procedure . 

( 2 ) Subject to the above , these persons bo reglarised . Shri 
Ashok Kaduskar , Shri Bhika Bhai Thakar and Shri S . P . 
Mchtani with effect from 1st October , 1971, and Miss P , A . 
Mary and Shri Bipal R . Tabkar with effect from 1st April 
1972 .

demand .

request the Central Government Industrial Tribunal to whom

consent award in terms of this settlement.

JX . The Union strongly contended for the dust allowance 
for the workers in the Mining Establishment. The Manage 
ment refused to concede to this demand on principle . Tho 
Union hus, therefore , withdrawn the demand for dust allow 
ance also . 


X . Although the new payscules and fitments are to be 
effective from 1 - 1 - 1973 , the Management agree to pay 
an amount of Rs. 15 , representing the flat increaso , for tho 
month of December , 1972 to all permanent workers who are 
on the rolls on the date of this agreement. 


XI. It is agiced between the parties that the fitment as 
the revised scales of pay will be given effect in the paysheets 
for the month of May 1973 and all artears arising out of this 
settlement will be paid along with the pay for May , 1973 . 
Bonus due and ex -gratia payment of 3 .67 per cent will, how 
ever , be paid along with tho salary of April , 1973 . 


XII. The Company will issue individual slips to each work 
man showing therein bis revised pay and allowance with 
effect from 1 - 1 - 1973 . 


XIII. Tho Management is further plcased to accord formal 
recognition as provided under thc Code of Discipline to Chow 
gule Employees Union . 


XIV . It is agreed between the parties that in respect of all 
points covered in this settlement and the settlement dated 
24 - 2 - 1972 which have the effect of modifying any provisions 
of the existing standing orders of the Company, the parties 
hereby agree to jointly approach before 15- 7 -73 , thc Certifying 
Officer to incorporate the said changes in the existing Standing 
Orders . 


XV . The Union on behalf of the workmen without pre 
judice to their rights and contentions, angures the Management 
good behaviour on the part of the workmen and that they 
would carry on their work in the customury manner , on 
resumption .

का निम्नलिखित प्रारूप , जिसे केन्द्रीय सरकार डाक कर्मकार 
(नियोजन का विनियमन ) अधिनियम , 1948 (1948 का 9) की 
धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए बनाने की प्रस्थापना करती है , उक्त उपधारा वारा यथाअपेक्षित , 
उन सभी व्यक्तियों की जानकारी के लिए जिनका उससे प्रभावित 
होना संभाव्य है , प्रकाशित किया जाता है , और यह सूचना दी 
जाती है कि उक्त प्रारूप पर, राजपत्र में इस अधिसूचना के 
प्रकाशन की तारीख से दो मास की अवधि की समाप्ति पर या 
उसके पश्चात विचार किया जाएगा । 

किन्हीं आक्षेपों या सुझावों पर , जो इस प्रकार विनिर्दिष्ट 
अर्धाध की समाप्ति के पूर्व उक्त प्रारूप की बात किसी व्यक्ति 
से प्राप्त किए जाएं केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा विचार किया जाएगा ।

आवंश 
का . आ . 2173. – यतः व्यास सतलज लिंक प्रोजेन्ट सन्दरनगर 
से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व 
ब्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ, सन्वरनगर करता है , ने संयुक्त 
रूप से केन्द्रीय सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि वह उनके बीच 
विद्यमान औद्योगिक विवाद को उक्त आवेदन में उपवर्णित और 
इससे उपायध अनुसूची में उद्धृत विषय के बारे में किसी आंद 
योगिक अधिकरण को निर्देशित कर । 

और यत्त : केन्द्रीय सरकार का समाधान हो गया है कि उक्त 
ज्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ , सन्दरनगर, कर्मकारों की बहसंख्या 
का प्रतिनिधित्व करता है । 


आदेश 
का . आ . 2174. -- यतः घ्यास सतलज लिंक प्रोजेक्ट सुन्दरनगर 
से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व 
ज्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ , सुन्दरनगर करता है , ने संयक्त 
रूप से केन्द्रीय सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि यह उनके बीच 
विद्यमान औद्योगिक विवाद को उक्त आवेदन में उपर्णित और 
इससे उपाषद्ध अनसूची में उद्धृत विषय के बारे में किसी आंद 
योगिक अधिकरण को निर्देशित करे । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार का समाधान हो गया है कि उक्त 
व्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ , सुन्दरनगर, कर्मकारों की बहसंख्या 
का प्रतिनिधित्व करता है , 

अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 (1947 का 
14) की धारा 7-क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 2 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एक औद्योगिक 
अधिकरण गठित करती हैं , जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री एच. आर . 
सोढी होंगे , जिनका मुख्यालय चण्डीगढ़ होगा और उक्स विवाद 
को उक्त औद्योगिक अधिकरण को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करती है । 


अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विषाद अधिनियम , 1947 (1947 का 
14 ) की धारा 7- 2 और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 2 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एक ऑयागिक 
अधिकरण गठित करती है , जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री एच . आर. 
सोढी होंगे, जिनका मुख्यालय चण्डीगढ होगा और उक्त विवाद 

को उक्त औद्योगिक अधिकरण को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निशित करती है ।

आवंश 


S . O . 2177. - .In pursuance of section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby publishes the following award of the Central Govern 
ment Industrial Tribunal, Madras in the industrial dispute 
between the employers in relation to the management of 
South Indian Bank Limited and their workmen which was 
rcceived by the Central Government on the 20th July , 1973 . 


___ का आ . 2178.-- यतः व्यास सतलज लिंक प्रोजेक्ट सन्दरनगर 
से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों जिनका प्रतिनिधिस्व 
घ्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ , सन्दरनगर करता है , ने संयुक्त 
रूप से केन्द्रीय सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि यह उनके बीच 
विद्यमान औद्योगिक विवाद को उक्त आवेदन में उपर्णित और 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में उद्धृत विषय के बारे में किसी ऑन 
योगिक अधिकरण को निर्देशित कर । 


BEFORE THIRU G . GOPINATH, B. A., B. L.., 

PRESIDING OFFICER 
INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL , MADRAS. 
(Constituted by the Central Governnent ) 

Friday , the 13th day of July , 1973 


Industrial Dispute No. 24 of 1973

व्यास सतलज लिंक कर्मकार संघ , सुन्दरनगर, कर्मकारों की यह संख्या 
का प्रतिनिधित्व करता है ;
